import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import {PluginHost} from '../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js';

async function fixture(t){
 const root=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'d1-fault-persist-')),hosts=[];
 t.after(async()=>{for(const host of hosts)await host.close();await fs.rm(root,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:8,retryDelay:50});});
 const entry=path.join(root,'provider.mjs');
 await fs.writeFile(entry,'export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke:()=>process.pid});');
 const host=()=>{const h=new PluginHost({stateRoot:root,deviceId:'fault-persist'});hosts.push(h);return h;};
 const runtime=host();
 await runtime.install({id:'provider',entry,version:'1.0.0',dataSchemaVersion:1,isolation:'process',capabilities:[{name:'fault.provider',version:'1.0.0'}]},{enabled:true});
 return {root,runtime,host};
}
function blockFaultSave(runtime){
 const save=runtime.save.bind(runtime);let blocked=true;
 runtime.save=async()=>{if(blocked)throw Object.assign(new Error('injected registry write failure'),{code:'EIO'});return save();};
 return ()=>{blocked=false;};
}
const fault=()=>Object.assign(new Error('fixture fault'),{code:'PLUGIN_FIXTURE_FAILED'});

test('failed fault persistence survives direct load and unrelated external revision without resurrecting child',async t=>{
 const {runtime,host}=await fixture(t);await runtime.start();clearInterval(runtime.refreshTimer);
 const pid=await runtime.invoke('fault.provider',{}),release=blockFaultSave(runtime);
 await runtime.markFault('provider',fault());
 await runtime.load();assert.equal(runtime.list()[0].state,'faulted','direct load discarded unpersisted fault');
 const external=host();await external.load();
 await external.install({...external.list()[0].manifest,id:'unrelated',capabilities:[]});
 release();await runtime.refresh();
 assert.equal(runtime.list().find(r=>r.manifest.id==='provider').state,'faulted');
 assert.deepEqual(runtime.capabilities(),[]);
 assert.throws(()=>process.kill(pid,0),{code:'ESRCH'});
 assert.equal(JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(runtime.registryPath,'utf8')).plugins.provider.state,'faulted');
 const restarted=host();await restarted.start();assert.deepEqual(restarted.capabilities(),[]);
});

test('failed activation save followed by failed fault save preserves sticky fault on reload',async t=>{
 const {runtime,host}=await fixture(t),release=blockFaultSave(runtime);
 await runtime.start();clearInterval(runtime.refreshTimer);
 assert.equal(runtime.registrySavePending,true);
 await runtime.markFault('provider',fault());await runtime.load();
 assert.equal(runtime.list()[0].state,'faulted');
 const external=host();await external.load();await external.install({...external.list()[0].manifest,id:'other',capabilities:[]});
 release();await runtime.refresh();assert.equal(runtime.list().find(r=>r.manifest.id==='provider').state,'faulted');
 assert.deepEqual(runtime.capabilities(),[]);
});

for(const action of ['enable','disable','uninstall'])test('unpersisted startup fault respects newer explicit '+action,async t=>{
 const {runtime,host}=await fixture(t);
 // The on-disk state is already installed/enabled. An explicit enable may write
 // the very same state: revision alone cannot distinguish it from another ID.
 const release=blockFaultSave(runtime);await runtime.markFault('provider',fault());
 const external=host();await external[action]('provider');release();
 await runtime.load();
 const record=runtime.list()[0];
 assert.equal(record.state,action==='enable'?'installed':action==='disable'?'disabled':'uninstalled');
 assert.equal(record.enabled,action==='enable');assert.equal(record.installed,action!=='uninstall');
 assert.equal(record.error,undefined);
});

test('POSIX registry rename syncs parent and keeps published revision after directory sync failure',async t=>{
 const {runtime}=await fixture(t),originalPlatform=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(process,'platform'),open=fs.open;
 let directoryOpens=0,closed=0,fail=true;
 fs.open=async function(filename,...args){
  if(filename===runtime.root){directoryOpens++;return {sync:async()=>{if(fail)throw Object.assign(new Error('directory sync failure'),{code:'EIO'});},close:async()=>{closed++;}};}
  return open.call(this,filename,...args);
 };
 Object.defineProperty(process,'platform',{value:'linux'});
 try{
  const before=runtime.registry.revision;
  await assert.rejects(runtime.save(),{code:'EIO'});
  assert.equal(runtime.registry.revision,before+1,'rename already published this revision');
  fail=false;await runtime.save();assert.equal(runtime.registry.revision,before+2);
  assert.equal(directoryOpens,2);assert.equal(closed,2);
 }finally{fs.open=open;Object.defineProperty(process,'platform',originalPlatform);}
});

test('actual Palace CLI process probe stays within 20 ms p95 incremental overhead',{timeout:180000},async t=>{
 const root=await tmp(t,'d1-cli-probe-'),home=path.join(root,'home'),data=path.join(root,'data');
 // Exercise installed package resolution, never the checkout early return. This
 // current-source fixture is not a new release candidate or tarball acceptance.
 const packageRoot=path.join(root,'node_modules','@hcu-lab.me','mcp-device');
 await fs.mkdir(packageRoot,{recursive:true});
 for(const name of ['dist','skills','package.json'])await fs.cp(path.resolve(name),path.join(packageRoot,name),{recursive:true});
 await assert.rejects(fs.stat(path.join(packageRoot,'src')),{code:'ENOENT'});
 const env=childEnv(home);env.MCP_DEVICE_SKILL_HOME=home;env.PALACE_DATA_ROOT=data;
 delete env.MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL;delete env.MCP_DEVICE_INSTALL_SKILLS_FORCE;
 const cli=path.join(packageRoot,'dist/plugins/palace/cli.js');
 const install=spawnSync(process.execPath,[cli,'skill','install'],{env,encoding:'utf8',timeout:15000});assert.equal(install.status,0,install.stderr);assert.equal(JSON.parse(install.stdout).ok,true);
 const ledgerPath=path.join(home,'.mcp-device/palace-skill/owners.json'),ledgerBefore=await fs.readFile(ledgerPath),ledger=JSON.parse(ledgerBefore);
 assert.equal(Object.values(ledger.owners).filter(x=>path.resolve(x.root)===path.resolve(packageRoot)).length,1);
 for(const target of [path.join(home,'.agents/skills/palace-cli'),path.join(home,'.pi/agent/skills/palace-cli')])assert.ok(ledger.targets[path.resolve(target)]);
 new PalaceStore(data,'d1-probe').close();
 const defender=observeDefender();
 const normal=[],skipped=[];
 const run=skip=>{
  const child={...env};if(skip)child.MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL='1';
  const started=performance.now(),out=spawnSync(process.execPath,[cli,'--data-root',data,'status'],{env:child,cwd:home,encoding:'utf8',timeout:10000});
  const elapsed=performance.now()-started;assert.equal(out.status,0,out.stderr);
  const body=JSON.parse(out.stdout);assert.equal(body.skillState,skip?'disabled':'present-recorded');assert.equal(body.skill.verification,'ledger-only');assert.equal(body.skill.observational,true);
  return elapsed;
 };
 // Alternate first arm to avoid always charging warm-up/drift to one arm.
 for(let i=0;i<200;i++){if(i%2){skipped.push(run(true));normal.push(run(false));}else{normal.push(run(false));skipped.push(run(true));}}
 assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(ledgerPath),ledgerBefore,'ordinary CLI calls must not mutate the ownership ledger');
 const percentile=values=>[...values].sort((a,b)=>a-b)[189],p95=percentile(normal),baselineP95=percentile(skipped),delta=p95-baselineP95;
 const receipt={samplesPerArm:200,p95Ms:p95,skipP95Ms:baselineP95,p95IncrementalMs:delta,acceptanceP95IncrementalMs:20,normalState:'present-recorded',skipState:'disabled',verification:'ledger-only',ledgerUnchanged:true,ledgerSha256:createHash('sha256').update(ledgerBefore).digest('hex'),packageRoot,install:'isolated installed-layout current-source fixture; not tarball/candidate acceptance',platform:process.platform,node:process.version,defender,normalMs:normal,skippedMs:skipped};
 if(process.env.MCP_V12_PROBE_RECEIPT)await fs.writeFile(process.env.MCP_V12_PROBE_RECEIPT,JSON.stringify(receipt,null,2)+'\n');
 t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify(receipt));
 assert.ok(delta<=20,`actual CLI probe p95 incremental overhead ${delta.toFixed(2)} ms exceeded 20 ms`);
});

import test from 'node:test';

const settings={AntivirusEnabled:true,RealTimeProtectionEnabled:true,DisableRealtimeMonitoring:false,ExclusionPath:'N/A: Must be an administrator to view exclusions',ExclusionProcess:'N/A: Must be an administrator to view exclusions'};

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/helpers/defender-context.mjs | original-bytes=1385 | sha256=20c348a1edc75ba7cf452bac44526ddf8aa4655f1a299aceca673442e310121b =====
import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';

// Windows MF-6 requires active Defender during the fresh-process measurement.
export function observeDefender(platform=process.platform,run=spawnSync){
 if(platform!=='win32')return {available:false,reason:'not Windows'};
 const observed=run('powershell.exe',['-NoProfile','-NonInteractive','-Command',"$s=Get-MpComputerStatus -ErrorAction Stop;$p=Get-MpPreference -ErrorAction Stop;[PSCustomObject]@{AntivirusEnabled=$s.AntivirusEnabled;RealTimeProtectionEnabled=$s.RealTimeProtectionEnabled;AMProductVersion=$s.AMProductVersion;AntivirusSignatureVersion=$s.AntivirusSignatureVersion;DisableRealtimeMonitoring=$p.DisableRealtimeMonitoring;ExclusionPath=$p.ExclusionPath;ExclusionProcess=$p.ExclusionProcess}|ConvertTo-Json -Compress"],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:15000,windowsHide:true});
 let defender;
 try{defender={available:observed.status===0,settings:JSON.parse(observed.stdout)};}
 catch{defender={available:false,exit:observed.status,error:observed.stderr};}
 if(!defender.available||defender.settings?.AntivirusEnabled!==true||defender.settings?.RealTimeProtectionEnabled!==true||defender.settings?.DisableRealtimeMonitoring!==false)
  throw Object.assign(new Error('Windows CLI probe requires observable active Defender and real-time protection; no samples collected.'),{code:'DEFENDER_REQUIRED',defender});
 return defender;
}

async function fixture(t) {
  const dir=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'palace-cli-contract-'));
  const logs=path.join(dir,'logs'),dataRoot=path.join(dir,'data');

  const found=await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'CLI_BUDGET_MARKER'});
  assert.equal(found.items.length,1);
  const run=(args)=>spawnSync(process.execPath,[cli,'--data-root',dataRoot,...args],{cwd:root,encoding:'utf8',timeout:10000});
  return {run,provider,id:found.items[0].id};
}

// The break is successful stdout exceeding the caller's byte budget after JSON indentation.
for(const command of ['search','read']) {
  test('CLI '+command+' bounds the actual UTF-8 stdout including its newline',async t=>{
    const f=await fixture(t),args=command==='search'?['search','CLI_BUDGET_MARKER']:['read',f.id];
    const full=f.run([...args,'--max-bytes','65536']);
    assert.equal(full.status,0,full.stderr);
    const budget=Math.max(512,Buffer.byteLength(JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(full.stdout)),'utf8')+1);
    assert.ok(budget<=65536);
    const bounded=f.run([...args,'--max-bytes',String(budget)]);
    if(bounded.status===0) {
      assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(bounded.stdout,'utf8')<=budget,
        'successful stdout must fit --max-bytes: '+Buffer.byteLength(bounded.stdout)+' > '+budget);
      assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(bounded.stdout),JSON.parse(full.stdout));
    } else {
      assert.equal(bounded.stdout,'');
      assert.match(bounded.stderr,/"code":"BUDGET_TOO_SMALL"/);
    }
  });
}

// The break is treating a query with no lexical terms as a request to list every card.
test('punctuation-only CLI search abstains like the canonical provider',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t);
  const canonical=await f.provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'!!!'});
  assert.equal(canonical.items.length,0);
  const result=f.run(['search','!!!']);
  assert.equal(result.status,0,result.stderr);
  assert.equal(JSON.parse(result.stdout).returned,0);
  assert.deepEqual(JSON.parse(result.stdout).items,[]);
});

test('CLI live read leaves the writer in control and explicitly disclaims fresh evidence verification',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t);
  const result=f.run(['read',f.id]);
  assert.equal(result.status,0,result.stderr);
  const selected=JSON.parse(result.stdout);
  assert.equal(selected.evidenceVerifiedNow,false);
  assert.equal(selected.mode,'read-only');
  await f.provider.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  const after=await f.provider.invoke('knowledge.read',{ref:f.id,evidence:true});
  assert.equal(after.card.id,f.id);
  assert.equal(after.evidence[0].verifiedBytes,true);
});

import test from 'node:test';

test('installer auto-registers Palace Skill without clobbering user-owned skill and is idempotent',async t=>{
  const home=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'palace-installer-'));
  t.after(async()=>fs.rm(home,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:5,retryDelay:50}));

  const env={...process.env,MCP_DEVICE_INSTALL_SKILLS_FORCE:'1',MCP_DEVICE_SKILL_HOME:home};
  const run=()=> {
    const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[installer],{cwd:root,env,encoding:'utf8'});
    assert.equal(result.status,0,result.stderr);
    return JSON.parse(result.stdout.trim());
  };
  const first=run();
  assert.equal(first.ok,true);
  assert.equal(first.result.find(x=>x.target===custom).state,'preserved-existing');

  const installed=path.join(home,'.agents','skills','palace-cli');
  assert.match(await fs.readFile(path.join(installed,'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),/^---/);
  const again=run();
  assert.equal(again.ok,true);
  assert.equal(again.result.find(x=>x.target===installed).state,'already-installed');
  const disabled=spawnSync(process.execPath,[installer],{cwd:root,env:{...env,MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL:'1'},encoding:'utf8'});

});

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/palace-package-types.test.mjs | original-bytes=3235 | sha256=4a63f774da07f94ee44fbceb72a9786c50e122620ed213c18af29e6990da3c2c =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import path from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
import { spawnSync } from 'node:child_process';
import ts from 'typescript';
import os from 'node:os';
import { verifyPackageImports } from '../../scripts/v12/palace-package-closure.mjs';

const root=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../',import.meta.url));
test('standalone public plugin types resolve without the Device package',async()=>{
  const built=spawnSync(process.execPath,[path.join(root,'scripts/v12/build-palace-package.mjs')],{cwd:root,encoding:'utf8',timeout:30000});
  assert.equal(built.status,0,built.stderr);
  const {output}=JSON.parse(built.stdout);
  const pending=[path.join(output,'dist/plugins/palace/index.d.ts'),path.join(output,'dist/plugins/palace/plugin.d.ts')],seen=new Set();
  while(pending.length){
    const file=pending.pop();if(seen.has(file))continue;seen.add(file);
    const text=await fs.readFile(file,'utf8');
    const imports=ts.preProcessFile(text,true,true).importedFiles;
    for(const item of imports){
      if(!item.fileName.startsWith('.'))continue;
      const resolved=ts.resolveModuleName(item.fileName,file,{module:ts.ModuleKind.NodeNext,moduleResolution:ts.ModuleResolutionKind.NodeNext},ts.sys).resolvedModule;
      assert.ok(resolved,`Missing public declaration dependency ${path.relative(output,file)} -> ${item.fileName}`);
      const rel=path.relative(output,resolved.resolvedFileName);
      assert.ok(!rel.startsWith('..')&&!path.isAbsolute(rel),`Declaration escapes standalone tarball: ${rel}`);
      if(resolved.resolvedFileName.endsWith('.d.ts'))pending.push(resolved.resolvedFileName);
    }
  }
  assert.ok(seen.size>=3,'The test must inspect the exported declarations and their dependencies.');
});

async function fixture(t){
  const dir=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'palace-closure-'));
  t.after(()=>fs.rm(dir,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:5,retryDelay:30}));
  const pkg=path.join(dir,'package');await fs.mkdir(pkg);return {dir,pkg};
}
test('closure rejects imports escaping the package even when the external file exists',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t);await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.dir,'outside.js'),'export const x=1;');
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.pkg,'index.js'),"export {x} from '../outside.js';");
  const result=await verifyPackageImports(f.pkg);
  assert.equal(result.ok,false);assert.equal(result.unresolved[0].reason,'import-escapes-package');
});
test('closure uses syntax nodes rather than matching import text inside comments and strings',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t);await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.pkg,'index.js'),"// import missing from './missing.js'\nexport const example=\"import('also-missing')\";\n");
  assert.equal((await verifyPackageImports(f.pkg)).ok,true);
});
test('computed runtime imports require an explicitly reviewed path contract',async t=>{
  const f=await fixture(t);await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.pkg,'index.js'),'export const load=name=>import(name);');
  const result=await verifyPackageImports(f.pkg);
  assert.equal(result.ok,false);assert.equal(result.unresolved[0].reason,'unreviewed-dynamic-import');
});

test('packed adapter rejects unconfigured or malformed trusted validation modules',async t=>{
  const root=await fixture(t),{createPlugin}=await load('plugin.js');
  const context={deviceId:'validation-contract',dataRoot:path.join(root,'data'),sourceRoots:[],projectRoots:[],settings:{autoIndex:false,discoverKnownSources:false,autoActivateSkills:true}};
  await assert.rejects(createPlugin(context),/TRUSTED_VALIDATION_MODULE_REQUIRED/);
  await assert.rejects(createPlugin({...context,settings:{...context.settings,validationModule:'relative-validator.mjs'}}),/TRUSTED_VALIDATION_MODULE_REQUIRED/);
  const module=path.join(root,'invalid.mjs');await fs.writeFile(module,'export const unrelated=true;\n');
  await assert.rejects(createPlugin({...context,settings:{...context.settings,validationModule:module}}),/VALIDATION_FACTORY_REQUIRED/);
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(root,'invalid-runner.mjs'),'export const createValidationRunner=()=>42;\n');
  await assert.rejects(createPlugin({...context,settings:{...context.settings,validationModule:path.join(root,'invalid-runner.mjs')}}),/VALIDATION_RUNNER_REQUIRED/);
});

test('packed adapter only activates through configured execution and independent postcondition',async t=>{
  const root=await fixture(t),logs=path.join(root,'logs'),output=path.join(root,'postcondition.txt'),module=path.join(root,'validator.mjs');await fs.mkdir(logs);
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(logs,'a.log'),'Error PACKED_VALIDATION_MARKER: preserve independently checked output.\n');
  await fs.writeFile(module,`import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';import fs from 'node:fs';export const createValidationRunner=()=>async()=>{const output=${JSON.stringify(output)};const run=spawnSync(process.execPath,['-e','require("node:fs").writeFileSync(process.argv[1],"verified")',output],{encoding:'utf8'});return {executionId:'packed-execution',exitCode:run.status,postconditionPassed:fs.readFileSync(output,'utf8')==='verified',output:'independently checked',validatorVersion:'packed-contract/1'};};\n`);
  const {createPlugin}=await load('plugin.js'),plugin=await createPlugin({deviceId:'packed-validator',dataRoot:path.join(root,'data'),sourceRoots:[logs],projectRoots:[],settings:{autoIndex:false,discoverKnownSources:false,autoActivateSkills:true,validationModule:module}});
  await plugin.start();const context={signal:new AbortController().signal};
  try{
    await plugin.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{},context);
    const found=await plugin.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'PACKED_VALIDATION_MARKER'},context);assert.equal(found.items.length,1);
    const learned=await plugin.invoke('knowledge.learn',{summary:'Packed validation boundary',steps:['Execute the independently checked fixture'],evidenceRefs:[found.items[0].id]},context);
    assert.equal(learned.activated,true);assert.equal(learned.card.status,'validated');assert.equal(await fs.readFile(output,'utf8'),'verified');
  }finally{await plugin.stop();}
});

test('provider candidate catalogue excludes withdrawn procedures after correction',async t=>{
  const root=await fixture(t),logs=path.join(root,'logs');await fs.mkdir(logs);
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(logs,'a.log'),'Error WITHDRAWAL_MARKER: previous remedy must be withdrawn after correction.\n');
  const {createPalaceProvider}=await load('index.js');
  const provider=createPalaceProvider({deviceId:'withdrawal-contract',dataRoot:path.join(root,'data'),sourceRoots:[logs],projectRoots:[],autoIndex:false,discoverKnownSources:false});
  await provider.start();t.after(()=>provider.stop());await provider.invoke('knowledge.ingest',{});
  const source=(await provider.invoke('knowledge.search',{query:'WITHDRAWAL_MARKER'})).items[0];assert.ok(source);
  const learned=await provider.invoke('knowledge.learn',{summary:'Withdrawable candidate',steps:['Use the prior remedy'],evidenceRefs:[source.id],autoActivate:false});
  assert.equal((await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{includeCandidates:true})).items.length,1);
  const corrected=await provider.invoke('knowledge.correct',{id:source.id,body:'The prior remedy is contradicted.',reason:'Independent negative case'});assert.ok(corrected.invalidated.includes(learned.card.id));
  try{
    const candidates=await provider.invoke('knowledge.skills',{includeCandidates:true});
    assert.equal(candidates.items.some(item=>item.id===learned.card.id),false,'withdrawn procedures must not appear in the candidate catalogue');
    assert.equal((await provider.invoke('knowledge.read',{id:learned.card.id})).card.status,'withdrawn','historical exact read remains auditable');
  }finally{await provider.stop();}
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import { rankCandidates } from '../../dist/plugins/palace/retrieval.js';

const card=(id,body)=>({
  id,version:1,contentHash:'x',summary:'Source claim: '+body,body,status:'observed',
  originDeviceId:'fixture',servingDeviceId:'fixture',scope:{visibility:'account'},
  evidence:[],derivedFrom:[],provenance:[],updatedAt:1,lastVerifiedAt:1
});

test('long paraphrase keeps a candidate with two independent lexical signals',()=>{
  const target=card('port','Error EADDRINUSE starting the isolated gateway: the chosen port already has a listener. Use the assigned test port after inspecting its owner. Production and backup listeners must not be terminated.');
  const distractor=card('proxy','Decision: a reverse proxy must forward WebSocket upgrades and preserve the original Host and authorization headers.');
  const query='A test gateway cannot bind its socket because another service owns the address; how do we investigate safely?';
  assert.deepEqual(rankCandidates([target,distractor],query,5).map(x=>x.id),['port']);
});

test('long no-answer query still abstains on a single generic lexical overlap',()=>{
  const node=card('node','Error node:sqlite import failed under Node 20 in the order worker. The package requires Node 22.13 or newer.');
  const query='How do I repair a Rust Cargo checksum mismatch in the package registry?';
  assert.deepEqual(rankCandidates([node],query,5),[]);
});

test('long no-answer query does not combine unrelated single-term cards',()=>{
  const image=card('image','Error image thumbnail conversion: preserve the original aspect ratio.');
  const auth=card('auth','Decision: credentials must be checked before authorization.');
  const query='How should a Kubernetes pod recover from an expired image pull credential?';
  assert.deepEqual(rankCandidates([image,auth],query,5),[]);
});

async function packageRoot(parent,name,version,body){
  const root=path.join(parent,name.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi,'-')+'-'+crypto.randomUUID());
  await fs.mkdir(path.join(root,'skills','palace-cli'),{recursive:true});

  await fs.writeFile(path.join(root,'skills','palace-cli','SKILL.md'),body);
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(root,'skills','palace-cli','agents.json'),'{}\n');
  return root;
}

});

  await reconcilePalaceSkill({home,packageRoot:pkg,force:true});

  await fs.writeFile(path.join(pkg,'package.json'),JSON.stringify({name:'@hcu-lab.me/mcp-device',version:'1.0.12',type:'module'}));await fs.writeFile(path.join(pkg,'skills','palace-cli','SKILL.md'),'# new\n');
  const result=await reconcilePalaceSkill({home,packageRoot:pkg,force:true}),item=result.result.find(value=>value.target===target(home));
  assert.equal(item.state,'preserved-modified');assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(target(home),'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),'# user edit\n');assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(target(home),'notes.txt'),'utf8'),'keep\n');
  assert.equal(item.pending.startsWith(path.join(home,'.mcp-device','palace-skill')),true);assert.equal(item.pending.includes(path.join('.agents','skills')),false);
  assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(item.pending,'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),'# new\n');
});

test('package relocation repairs the Skill without retaining a dangling package link',async t=>{

test('pending staged publish recovers after interruption without exposing a partial Skill',async t=>{

  await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(aside),{recursive:true});await fs.rename(target(home),aside);
  ledger.pending={id,target:target(home),stage,aside,selectedOwner:record.selectedOwner,version:record.version,files:record.files};await fs.writeFile(ledgerPath,JSON.stringify(ledger,null,2));
  await reconcilePalaceSkill({home,packageRoot:pkg,force:true});assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(target(home),'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),'# desired\n');assert.equal((await palaceSkillStatus({home,packageRoot:pkg})).result[0].state,'owned-pristine');

const digest=b=>createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');
async function fixture(t){
 const root=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'palace-probe-'));t.after(()=>fs.rm(root,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:8,retryDelay:50}));

test('metadata probes do not hash source bodies, publish, acquire locks, or change the ledger',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.source,'large-reference.txt'),'x'.repeat(2*1024*1024));await f.run();
 const before=await fs.readFile(f.ledgerPath),original={};const forbidden=[];
 for(const name of ['writeFile','rename','cp','rm','mkdir','open']){original[name]=fs[name];fs[name]=async(...args)=>{forbidden.push(name);throw new Error('Read-only probe attempted '+name);};}
 original.readFile=fs.readFile;fs.readFile=async(filename,...args)=>{
   if(path.resolve(String(filename)).startsWith(path.resolve(f.source)+path.sep))throw new Error('Read-only probe hashed source content');
   return original.readFile(filename,...args);
 };
 const timings=[],baseline=[];let children=0;const hook=createHook({init(_id,type){if(type==='PROCESSWRAP')children++;}}).enable();
 const skip=process.env.MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL;
 try{
  for(let i=0;i<200;i++){
   delete process.env.MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL;
   let start=performance.now();const result=await palaceSkillProbe(f.options);timings.push(performance.now()-start);
   assert.equal(result.state,'present-recorded');assert.equal(result.verification,'metadata-only');assert.equal(result.observational,true);
   process.env.MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL='1';start=performance.now();await palaceSkillProbe(f.options);baseline.push(performance.now()-start);
  }
 }finally{hook.disable();for(const [name,method] of Object.entries(original))fs[name]=method;if(skip===undefined)delete process.env.MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL;else process.env.MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL=skip;}
 assert.deepEqual(forbidden,[]);assert.equal(children,0);assert.equal(digest(await fs.readFile(f.ledgerPath)),digest(before));
 timings.sort((a,b)=>a-b);baseline.sort((a,b)=>a-b);
 const p95Delta=timings[189]-baseline[189];
 t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify({samples:timings.length,p50Ms:timings[99],p95Ms:timings[189],maxMs:timings.at(-1),baselineP95Ms:baseline[189],p95IncrementalMs:p95Delta,measurement:'in-process cache diagnostic only; actual CLI process latency is asserted separately',bytesInSourceTree:2*1024*1024,sourceBodyReads:0,filesystemWrites:0,children}));
});

import assert from 'node:assert/strict';

test('Palace Skill uses one bounded recall before broad incident inspection and abstains on no match',async()=>{
  const text=await fs.readFile(skillPath,'utf8');
  assert.match(text,/before broad workspace\/archive\/log inspection/i);
  assert.match(text,/run one bounded `palace search`/i);
  assert.match(text,/If nothing relevant is returned[\s\S]*do not keep searching Palace/i);
  assert.match(text,/exact `validated` procedure[\s\S]*avoid a broad archive scan/i);
  assert.match(text,/Verify the current target\/postcondition independently/i);
  assert.match(text,/Do not recall for self-contained formatting\/limits or clearly novel work/i);
});

async function interrupted(f,{corruptStage=false,editAside=false}={}){
 await f.run();const ledger=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(f.ledgerPath,'utf8')),record=ledger.targets[f.target],id=randomUUID();
 const key=createHash('sha256').update(path.resolve(f.target)).digest('hex').slice(0,24);
 const base=path.dirname(f.ledgerPath),stage=path.join(base,'staging',id,key),aside=path.join(base,'aside',id,key);
 await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(stage),{recursive:true});await fs.cp(f.source,stage,{recursive:true});
 await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(aside),{recursive:true});await fs.rename(f.target,aside);
 if(corruptStage)await fs.writeFile(path.join(stage,'SKILL.md'),'partial');
 if(editAside)await fs.writeFile(path.join(aside,'my-notes.txt'),'user edit during interrupted upgrade');
 ledger.pending={id,target:f.target,stage,aside,selectedOwner:record.selectedOwner,version:record.version,files:record.files};
 await fs.writeFile(f.ledgerPath,JSON.stringify(ledger));return {stage,aside};
}

test('interrupted update recovery does not publish an unchecked partial staging tree',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await interrupted(f,{corruptStage:true});
 await f.run().catch(error=>assert.match(error.message,/SKILL|RECOVERY/));
 const body=await fs.readFile(path.join(f.target,'SKILL.md'),'utf8').catch(()=>null);
 assert.notEqual(body,'partial');assert.equal(body,'# original\n','complete prior generation must be restored');
});

test('user edits to an aside tree survive completion of an interrupted publish',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t),pending=await interrupted(f,{editAside:true});await f.run();
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(pending.aside,'my-notes.txt'),'utf8'),'user edit during interrupted upgrade');
});

test('recovery validates managed paths before touching any ledger-supplied path',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await f.run();const ledger=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(f.ledgerPath,'utf8')),record=ledger.targets[f.target];
 const foreign=path.join(f.root,'must-not-delete');await fs.mkdir(foreign);await fs.writeFile(path.join(foreign,'notes.txt'),'keep');
 ledger.pending={id:randomUUID(),target:f.target,stage:path.join(f.root,'missing-stage'),aside:foreign,selectedOwner:record.selectedOwner,version:record.version,files:record.files};
 await fs.writeFile(f.ledgerPath,JSON.stringify(ledger));await assert.rejects(f.run(),/SKILL|RECOVERY/);
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(foreign,'notes.txt'),'utf8'),'keep');
});

import test from 'node:test';

import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import {randomUUID,createHash} from 'node:crypto';
import {spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {reconcilePalaceSkill,palaceSkillStatus} from '../../dist/plugins/palace/skill-manager.js';
import {PalaceStore} from '../../dist/plugins/palace/store.js';

async function fixture(t){
 const root=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'palace-opus-regression-'));
 t.after(()=>fs.rm(root,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:6,retryDelay:50}));
 const home=path.join(root,'home'),state=path.join(home,'.mcp-device','palace-skill');
 const target=path.join(home,'.agents','skills','palace-cli'),ledgerPath=path.join(state,'owners.json');
 async function pkg(label,name='@hcu-lab.me/palace',version='1.0.12'){
  const dir=path.join(root,label,'node_modules',...name.split('/'));
  await fs.mkdir(path.join(dir,'skills','palace-cli'),{recursive:true});
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(dir,'package.json'),JSON.stringify({name,version}));
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(dir,'skills','palace-cli','SKILL.md'),label+' '+version+'\n');return dir;
 }
 const packageRoot=await pkg('initial');
 const run=(options={})=>reconcilePalaceSkill({home,packageRoot,...options});
 const ledger=()=>fs.readFile(ledgerPath,'utf8').then(JSON.parse);
 async function count(dir){let n=0;try{for(const e of await fs.readdir(dir,{withFileTypes:true})){n++;if(e.isDirectory())n+=await count(path.join(dir,e.name));}}catch(e){if(e.code!=='ENOENT')throw e;}return n;}
 return {root,home,state,target,ledgerPath,packageRoot,pkg,run,ledger,count};
}

test('M1 other package owners refresh after an upgrade with install scripts disabled',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t),other=await f.pkg('other','@hcu-lab.me/mcp-device','1.0.13');
 await f.run();await f.run({packageRoot:other});
 await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.target,'notes.txt'),'preserve');
 await fs.writeFile(path.join(other,'package.json'),JSON.stringify({name:'@hcu-lab.me/mcp-device',version:'1.0.14'}));
 await fs.writeFile(path.join(other,'skills','palace-cli','SKILL.md'),'other changed offline\n');
 const first=await f.run(),offered=first.result.find(x=>x.target===f.target).pending;
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(offered,'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),'other changed offline\n');
 const count=await f.count(f.state);await f.run();await f.run();assert.equal(await f.count(f.state),count);
 assert.ok(Object.values((await f.ledger()).owners).some(x=>x.version==='1.0.14'));
});

test('M2 package aliases identify one owner and share the same removal preference',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await f.run();const alias=path.join(f.root,'package-alias');
 await fs.symlink(f.packageRoot,alias,process.platform==='win32'?'junction':'dir');
 await f.run({packageRoot:alias,force:true});assert.equal(Object.keys((await f.ledger()).owners).length,1);
 const alternate=process.platform==='win32'?f.packageRoot.toUpperCase():alias;
 await f.run({packageRoot:alternate,force:true,removeCurrentOwner:true});
 await f.run();await assert.rejects(fs.lstat(f.target),{code:'ENOENT'});
});

test('M3 an old empty lock reports recovery required and explicit repair can recover it',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await fs.mkdir(f.state,{recursive:true});const lock=path.join(f.state,'owners.lock');
 await fs.writeFile(lock,'');await fs.utimes(lock,new Date(0),new Date(0));
 await assert.rejects(f.run(),error=>error.code==='PALACE_SKILL_LOCK_RECOVERY_REQUIRED');
 await f.run({force:true,breakLock:true});assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(f.target,'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),'initial 1.0.12\n');
});

test('M3 recovery never steals a live owner lock even when explicitly requested',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await fs.mkdir(f.state,{recursive:true});const lock=path.join(f.state,'owners.lock');
 const bytes=JSON.stringify({pid:process.pid,token:randomUUID()});await fs.writeFile(lock,bytes);
 await assert.rejects(f.run({force:true,breakLock:true}),error=>['PALACE_SKILL_LOCK_ACTIVE','PALACE_SKILL_BUSY'].includes(error.code));
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(lock,'utf8'),bytes);
});

test('M4 corrupt ledgers remain intact until explicit lossless recovery',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await f.run();await fs.writeFile(f.ledgerPath,'{"interrupted":');
 await assert.rejects(f.run(),error=>error.code==='PALACE_SKILL_LEDGER_CORRUPT');
 const result=await f.run({force:true,abandonPending:true});
 const backups=(await fs.readdir(f.state)).filter(x=>x.startsWith('owners.json.corrupt-'));assert.equal(backups.length,1);
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(f.state,backups[0]),'utf8'),'{"interrupted":');
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(f.target,'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),'initial 1.0.12\n');
 // Explicit lossless reset can recover an unchanged JSON-marked copy only after
 // verifying all bytes against the installed package; it must not remain foreign.
 assert.equal(result.result.find(x=>x.target===f.target).state,'updated');
 const recovered=await f.ledger();assert.ok(recovered.targets[f.target]);
 assert.equal(recovered.targets[f.target].version,'1.0.12');
});

test('M4 missing stage for a new target is recoverable without a fictional prior tree',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await f.run();const ledger=await f.ledger(),record=ledger.targets[f.target];
 await fs.rename(f.target,path.join(f.root,'saved-test-copy'));delete ledger.targets[f.target];
 const id=randomUUID(),key=createHash('sha256').update(path.resolve(f.target)).digest('hex').slice(0,24);
 ledger.pending={id,target:f.target,stage:path.join(f.state,'staging',id,key),aside:path.join(f.state,'aside',id,key),hadPrior:false,...record};
 await fs.writeFile(f.ledgerPath,JSON.stringify(ledger));await f.run();
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(f.target,'SKILL.md'),'utf8'),'initial 1.0.12\n');assert.equal((await f.ledger()).pending,undefined);
});

test('M5 one unsafe agent-home parent does not block the other target',async t=>{

});

for(const kind of ['directory','live-link','dangling-link'])test('M6 explicit adoption preserves an unowned '+kind,async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(f.target),{recursive:true});const foreign=path.join(f.root,'foreign');
 if(kind==='directory') {await fs.mkdir(f.target);await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.target,'notes.txt'),'user bytes');}
 else {if(kind==='live-link'){await fs.mkdir(foreign);await fs.writeFile(path.join(foreign,'notes.txt'),'user bytes');}await fs.symlink(foreign,f.target,process.platform==='win32'?'junction':'dir');}
 const oldLink=kind==='directory'?null:await fs.readlink(f.target);
 const result=await f.run({force:true,adoptTarget:'agents'}),item=result.result.find(x=>x.target===f.target);
 assert.equal(item.state,'adopted');assert.ok(item.saved);assert.equal((await fs.lstat(f.target)).isSymbolicLink(),false);
 if(oldLink)assert.equal(await fs.readlink(item.saved),oldLink);else assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(item.saved,'notes.txt'),'utf8'),'user bytes');
 if(kind==='live-link')assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(foreign,'notes.txt'),'utf8'),'user bytes');
});

 store.atomic(()=>store.put({id:'card',version:1,contentHash:'fixture',summary:'needle',body:'needle',status:'observed',originDeviceId:'busy-fixture',servingDeviceId:'busy-fixture',scope:{visibility:'account'},evidence:[],derivedFrom:[],provenance:[],updatedAt:1,lastVerifiedAt:null}));store.close();
 const cli=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../dist/plugins/palace/cli.js',import.meta.url)),began=performance.now();
 const result=spawnSync(process.execPath,[cli,'--data-root',data,'search','needle'],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:10000,env:{...process.env,MCP_DEVICE_SKILL_HOME:f.home,MCP_DEVICE_INSTALL_SKILLS_FORCE:'1'}});
 const elapsedMs=performance.now()-began;assert.equal(result.status,0,result.stderr);
 assert.equal(JSON.parse(result.stdout).returned,1);assert.ok(elapsedMs<1500,'Skill lock delayed read: '+elapsedMs+'ms');
 t.diagnostic(JSON.stringify({elapsedMs}));
});

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import {randomUUID,createHash} from 'node:crypto';

import {PalaceStore} from '../../dist/plugins/palace/store.js';

const root=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../',import.meta.url));
const digest=bytes=>createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');
async function fixture(t){
 const root=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'palace-d1r2-'));
 t.after(()=>fs.rm(root,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:8,retryDelay:50}));
 const home=path.join(root,'home'),state=path.join(home,'.mcp-device/palace-skill'),target=path.join(home,'.agents/skills/palace-cli'),ledgerPath=path.join(state,'owners.json');
 async function pkg(label,name='@hcu-lab.me/palace',version='1.0.12'){
  const dir=path.join(root,label,'node_modules',...name.split('/'));await fs.mkdir(path.join(dir,'skills/palace-cli'),{recursive:true});
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(dir,'package.json'),JSON.stringify({name,version,type:'module'}));
  await fs.writeFile(path.join(dir,'skills/palace-cli/SKILL.md'),label+' '+version+'\n');return dir;
 }
 const packageRoot=await pkg('initial'),run=(options={})=>reconcilePalaceSkill({home,packageRoot,...options});
 const ledger=()=>fs.readFile(ledgerPath,'utf8').then(JSON.parse);
 async function leaves(dir){try{let out=[];for(const e of await fs.readdir(dir,{withFileTypes:true})){const file=path.join(dir,e.name);if(e.isDirectory())out.push(...await leaves(file));else out.push(file);}return out;}catch(error){if(error.code==='ENOENT')return [];throw error;}}
 return {root,home,state,target,ledgerPath,packageRoot,pkg,run,ledger,leaves};
}

test('D1 B1 repeated failed publication does not accumulate unmodified staging or pending intents',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);await f.run();
 const prior=await fs.readFile(path.join(f.target,'SKILL.md'));
 await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.packageRoot,'package.json'),JSON.stringify({name:'@hcu-lab.me/palace',version:'1.0.13'}));
 await fs.writeFile(path.join(f.packageRoot,'skills/palace-cli/SKILL.md'),'new package bytes\n');
 const rename=fs.rename;fs.rename=async(a,b)=>{if(path.resolve(a)===path.resolve(f.target))throw Object.assign(new Error('fixture cross-device rename refusal'),{code:'EXDEV'});return rename(a,b);};
 t.after(()=>{fs.rename=rename;});
 const seen=[];for(let i=0;i<50;i++)try{seen.push(await f.run());}catch(error){seen.push({error:error.code});}
 fs.rename=rename;
 assert.deepEqual(await fs.readFile(path.join(f.target,'SKILL.md')),prior);
 assert.deepEqual(await f.leaves(path.join(f.state,'staging')),[],'Unpublished staging must not grow on repeated failure');
 const ledger=await f.ledger();assert.equal(ledger.pending,undefined);
 assert.ok(Object.keys(ledger.retained??{}).length<=1,'Only the other successfully updated target may have a retained prior tree');
 assert.ok(seen.every(result=>result.result?.some(row=>row.target===f.target&&row.state==='publish-failed')),'Each refusal must be an actionable per-target publish-failed result');
});

test('D1 B2 old timestamps never authorize stealing a matching live lock holder',async t=>{
 const f=await fixture(t);let entered,release;const ready=new Promise(r=>{entered=r;}),gate=new Promise(r=>{release=r;});
 const cp=fs.cp;fs.cp=async(a,b,o)=>{if(path.resolve(a)===path.resolve(path.join(f.packageRoot,'skills/palace-cli'))){entered();await gate;}return cp(a,b,o);};
 t.after(()=>{fs.cp=cp;release();});
 const first=f.run();await ready;
 const lock=path.join(f.state,'owners.lock'),before=await fs.readFile(lock,'utf8');await fs.utimes(lock,new Date(0),new Date(0));
 await assert.rejects(f.run({force:true,breakLock:true}),error=>error.code==='PALACE_SKILL_LOCK_ACTIVE');
 assert.equal(await fs.readFile(lock,'utf8'),before);release();await first;fs.cp=cp;
});

===== BEGIN SOURCE test/v12/palace-wal-recovery.test.mjs | original-bytes=10691 | sha256=97dfec4b50fac41aafc28c630973e8e448697f63f5d827a7b439bfe418b4ec67 =====
import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import {spawn,spawnSync} from 'node:child_process';
import {createHash} from 'node:crypto';
import {pathToFileURL,fileURLToPath} from 'node:url';
import {PalaceStore} from '../../dist/plugins/palace/store.js';
import {PluginHost} from '../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js';
import {bundledPluginManifests} from '../../dist/distribution.js';

const packageRoot=fileURLToPath(new URL('../../',import.meta.url));
const cli=path.join(packageRoot,'dist/plugins/palace/cli.js');
const storeUrl=new URL('../../dist/plugins/palace/store.js',import.meta.url).href;
async function fixture(t){const root=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'palace-killed-wal-'));t.after(()=>fs.rm(root,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:8,retryDelay:50}));return root;}
async function durable(file){return Object.fromEntries(await Promise.all(['','-wal'].map(async suffix=>[suffix,createHash('sha256').update(await fs.readFile(file+suffix)).digest('hex')])));}
async function killWriter(t,root,schema){
 await fs.mkdir(root,{recursive:true});const file=path.join(root,'palace.sqlite');
 new PalaceStore(root,'wal-fixture').close();
 const code=`import{DatabaseSync}from'node:sqlite';const db=new DatabaseSync(${JSON.stringify(file)});db.exec("PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL;PRAGMA wal_autocheckpoint=0;BEGIN IMMEDIATE");db.prepare('UPDATE meta SET value=? WHERE key=?').run(${JSON.stringify(schema)},'schema');db.prepare('INSERT INTO meta VALUES(?,?)').run('committed','survives-kill');db.exec('COMMIT;BEGIN IMMEDIATE');db.prepare('INSERT INTO meta VALUES(?,?)').run('uncommitted','must-rollback');console.log('WAL_READY');setInterval(()=>{},1000);`;
 const child=spawn(process.execPath,['--input-type=module','-e',code],{stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe'],windowsHide:true});
 const exited=new Promise(resolve=>child.once('exit',resolve));t.after(async()=>{if(child.exitCode===null&&child.signalCode===null){child.kill('SIGKILL');await exited;}});
 await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let output='',stderr='';const timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error('writer readiness timeout: '+stderr)),5000);child.stderr.on('data',x=>stderr+=x);child.stdout.on('data',x=>{output+=x;if(output.includes('WAL_READY')){clearTimeout(timer);resolve();}});child.once('error',reject);child.once('exit',code=>{clearTimeout(timer);reject(new Error('writer exited before readiness: '+code+' '+stderr));});});
 child.kill('SIGKILL');await exited;assert.ok((await fs.stat(file+'-wal')).size>32,'writer must leave durable non-empty WAL');return file;
}
function reader(root){return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const child=spawn(process.execPath,[cli,'--data-root',root,'status'],{env:{...process.env,MCP_DEVICE_SKIP_SKILL_INSTALL:'1'},stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe'],windowsHide:true});let stdout='',stderr='';const timer=setTimeout(()=>{child.kill();reject(new Error('reader timeout'));},8000);child.stdout.on('data',x=>stdout+=x);child.stderr.on('data',x=>stderr+=x);child.once('error',reject);child.once('exit',code=>{clearTimeout(timer);resolve({code,stdout,stderr});});});}
for(const shm of ['stale','missing'])for(const schema of ['1','2'])test(`killed writer schema ${schema}, ${shm} SHM: concurrent read-only CLIs preserve durable WAL`,{timeout:20000},async t=>{
 const root=await fixture(t),file=await killWriter(t,root,schema);if(shm==='missing')await fs.rm(file+'-shm',{force:true});
 const before=await durable(file),results=await Promise.all(Array.from({length:4},()=>reader(root)));
 for(const result of results){assert.equal(result.code,schema==='1'?0:1,result.stderr);const output=JSON.parse(result.stdout||result.stderr.trim().split(/\r?\n/).filter(x=>x.startsWith('{')).at(-1));assert.equal(schema==='1'?output.ok:output.code,schema==='1'?true:'PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED');}
 assert.deepEqual(await durable(file),before,'read-only CLI schema inspection must not checkpoint durable bytes');
 if(schema==='2'){
  assert.throws(()=>new PalaceStore(root,'wal-reopen'),/PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED/);assert.deepEqual(await durable(file),before);
 }else{
  const runtime=path.join(root,'runtime'),data=path.join(runtime,'plugins/palace/data');await fs.mkdir(data,{recursive:true});
  for(const suffix of ['','-wal'])await fs.copyFile(file+suffix,path.join(data,'palace.sqlite')+suffix);
  const host=new PluginHost({stateRoot:runtime,deviceId:'wal-reopen'});
  try{
   await host.ensureBundled(bundledPluginManifests().filter(x=>x.id==='palace'));
   const started=performance.now();await host.start();assert.equal(host.list()[0].state,'enabled',host.list()[0].error);assert.ok(performance.now()-started<10000,'compatible process provider must initialize within its 10s default deadline');
   const reopened=new PalaceStore(root,'wal-reopen');try{assert.equal(reopened.getMeta('committed'),'survives-kill');assert.equal(reopened.getMeta('uncommitted'),'');}finally{reopened.close();}
  }finally{await host.close();}
 }
 assert.deepEqual((await fs.readdir(root)).filter(x=>x.startsWith('.schema-probe-')),[]);
});

test('crashed owned schema snapshots are reclaimed without touching active peers or unowned paths',{timeout:25000},async t=>{
 const root=await fixture(t),data=path.join(root,'data'),file=await killWriter(t,data,'2'),before=await durable(file);
 const shim=path.join(root,'cleanup-shim.mjs'),loader=path.join(root,'cleanup-loader.mjs');
 await fs.writeFile(shim,`import{DatabaseSync as Real}from'node:sqlite';export class DatabaseSync extends Real{constructor(file,options){if(options?.readOnly&&file===${JSON.stringify(file)})throw Object.assign(new Error('SQLITE_READONLY_RECOVERY'),{code:'SQLITE_READONLY_RECOVERY'});super(file,options);}}`);
 await fs.writeFile(loader,`export async function load(url,context,next){const result=await next(url,context);if(url===${JSON.stringify(storeUrl)})return {...result,source:String(result.source).replace("from 'node:sqlite'",${JSON.stringify('from '+JSON.stringify(pathToFileURL(shim).href))})};return result;}`);
 const prelude=`import fs from'node:fs';import{syncBuiltinESMExports}from'node:module';const copy=fs.copyFileSync;fs.copyFileSync=(...args)=>{copy(...args);console.log('SNAPSHOT_READY:'+args[1]);if(process.env.SNAPSHOT_MODE==='crash')process.exit(86);Atomics.wait(new Int32Array(new SharedArrayBuffer(4)),0,0);};syncBuiltinESMExports();const{PalaceStore}=await import(${JSON.stringify(storeUrl)});new PalaceStore(${JSON.stringify(data)},'cleanup');`;
 const crash=spawnSync(process.execPath,['--experimental-loader',pathToFileURL(loader).href,'--input-type=module','-e',prelude],{encoding:'utf8',timeout:10000,env:{...process.env,SNAPSHOT_MODE:'crash'}});
 assert.equal(crash.status,86,crash.stderr);const crashed=path.dirname(crash.stdout.trim().split('SNAPSHOT_READY:')[1]);assert.ok((await fs.stat(path.join(crashed,'palace.sqlite'))).size>0);
 const active=spawn(process.execPath,['--experimental-loader',pathToFileURL(loader).href,'--input-type=module','-e',prelude],{stdio:['ignore','pipe','pipe'],windowsHide:true,env:{...process.env,SNAPSHOT_MODE:'active'}}),exited=new Promise(resolve=>active.once('exit',resolve));
 t.after(async()=>{active.kill('SIGKILL');await exited;});
 const peer=await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{let text='',errors='';const timer=setTimeout(()=>reject(new Error('snapshot peer timeout: '+errors)),10000);active.stderr.on('data',x=>errors+=x);active.stdout.on('data',x=>{text+=x;if(text.includes('SNAPSHOT_READY:')){clearTimeout(timer);resolve(path.dirname(text.trim().split('SNAPSHOT_READY:')[1]));}});active.once('error',reject);});
 const unowned=path.join(data,'.schema-probe-unowned');await fs.mkdir(unowned);await fs.writeFile(path.join(unowned,'keep'),'not ours');
 assert.throws(()=>new PalaceStore(data,'cleanup-observer'),/PALACE_SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED/);
 assert.equal(await fs.stat(crashed).then(()=>true,()=>false),false,'crashed snapshot leaked copied knowledge');assert.ok(await fs.stat(peer));assert.equal(await fs.readFile(path.join(unowned,'keep'),'utf8'),'not ours');assert.deepEqual(await durable(file),before);
});

// Force only the OS-specific read-only-recovery failure, while all SQL and

import test from 'node:test';
import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
import fs from 'node:fs/promises';
import os from 'node:os';
import path from 'node:path';
import { PluginHost } from '../../dist/platform/plugin-host.js';

const sleep=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));

test('cancelling queued request B never kills unrelated running request A',{timeout:16000},async ()=>{
 const root=await fs.mkdtemp(path.join(os.tmpdir(),'d1-cancel-isolation-')),entry=path.join(root,'serial.mjs');
 await fs.writeFile(entry,`const delay=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));let tail=Promise.resolve();export const createPlugin=()=>({invoke(_n,input,{signal}){const run=tail.then(async()=>{if(input==='A'){await delay(6500);return 'A';}if(signal.aborted)throw Object.assign(new Error('cancelled before queued work'),{code:'CANCELLED'});return input;});tail=run.catch(()=>{});return run;}});`);
 const host=new PluginHost({stateRoot:root,deviceId:'d1'});
 try{
  await host.install({id:'serial',version:'1.0.0',dataSchemaVersion:1,isolation:'process',entry,timeoutMs:9000,capabilities:[{name:'d1.serial',version:'1.0.0'}]},{enabled:true});await host.start();
  const a=host.invoke('d1.serial','A');await sleep(100);
  const controller=new AbortController(),b=host.invoke('d1.serial','B',{signal:controller.signal});await sleep(900);
  const cancelledAt=Date.now();controller.abort();await assert.rejects(b,{code:'CANCELLED'});assert.ok(Date.now()-cancelledAt<200,'caller cancellation was not prompt');
  assert.equal(await a,'A');assert.equal(host.list()[0].state,'enabled');assert.equal(await host.invoke('d1.serial','C'),'C');
 }finally{
  await host.close().catch(()=>{});
  await fs.rm(root,{recursive:true,force:true,maxRetries:8,retryDelay:50});
 }
});

# Current status — MCP Device 1.0.12

Updated 2026-10-22. This is the v12 source candidate on `feat/v12-platform-20261022` in the isolated G6 checkout `E:\git-project\agent-mcp-device-v12`. The original Device and Gateway checkouts remain separate. No npm publication, production deployment, global Device replacement or G8 runtime change is claimed.

## Current increment

- Existing `start` remains Device-only; `device` is an alias. `server` composes the retained account/OAuth control plane with the same Device Core in process.
- Normal account, OAuth and Device pairing apply to localhost. Operator pages share the server listener and administrator policy.
- The generic versioned plugin host supports lifecycle, dependency services, composition and failure isolation. Palace is bundled/enabled by distribution policy; Core does not depend on it.
- Platform knowledge search/list return compact references without import. Selected read can lazily import into a compatible requester provider; a Device without Palace uses bounded session cache. Ownership, scope, origin, version, evidence and correction rules remain enforced.
- Palace discovers bounded known local sources offline, constructs knowledge and inactive procedure candidates, and requires trusted execution plus postconditions for learned Skill activation. It does not rewrite AGENTS.md.
- Stopped-state backup, verification, migration and restore preserve sensitive state and plugin evidence. Apply writes only a new target after verified backups.

## Evidence and limits

[Spec 008 acceptance](../specs/008-v12-platform/ACCEPTANCE.md) owns the final test/package receipts and remaining gaps. [Device inventory](../specs/008-v12-platform/device-parity.json) and [Gateway inventory](../specs/008-v12-platform/gateway-parity.json) separate baseline evidence, candidate results and NOT_RUN/PARTIAL items. The [v12 guide](v12-platform.md) documents commands and operating constraints.

The old Windows direct-entry test guards could exit without executing assertions. V12 parity executes the actual exported suites and records assertion-bearing baseline/candidate receipts. Historic aggregate counts must not be reused as new acceptance. Known baseline fixture gaps are recorded explicitly rather than hidden by candidate-only changes.

Retrieval remains lexical. The independent paraphrase holdout, task-replay measurements, response-byte costs and unmeasured workload limits are reported separately from the tuned regression corpus. No general token-saving or production workload claim follows from the synthetic fixtures.

Only G6 isolated port 8104 is used for server acceptance; the existing 8103 audit listener is preserved. G8 8102 is production and 8101 is backup, outside this work.

## Earlier status

The [2026-10-13 P1 snapshot](status-p1-20261013.md) is retained as historical context, including claims superseded by the executable v12 parity audit. Existing P1 `logs` and gated Context behavior remain compatibility surfaces; their historical capture approvals are not changed by enabling the separate Palace plugin.

===== BEGIN SOURCE docs/context.md | original-bytes=8002 | sha256=29019b2cfc3f46d1dd9096d1d564cb676fbb3d023acbebfb2a0c8fa1e3109f69 =====
# Device-local context — 1.0.11 (unreleased)

## Runtime and migration

Node **>=22.13.0** is required. Context uses built-in `node:sqlite`, WAL and FTS5; no external SQLite npm driver is needed. Validate the actual runtime with `node --test test/context/sqlite-platform.test.js`. SQLite may emit an experimental warning on supported Node 22 builds; that warning is not a test failure.

Upgrade Node before adopting this branch. Stop the runtime and back up the complete `~/.mcp-device/` directory (or configured state root), including WAL files, before migration. Store opening applies versioned local schema migrations; do not point an older runtime at a migrated context store. Legacy JSONL import is explicit/idempotent, preserves source files, and quarantines unknown owner/repository evidence rather than assigning it to the current account. There is no automatic legacy ownership adoption or rollback promise. Published 1.0.10 release history is unchanged.

## Local commands

Run on the device state host and inside the intended Git repository. A container or SSH workspace is not automatically the state host. `--cwd` and `--json` are shared flags. JSON is written to stdout; safe diagnostic codes go to stderr.

| Command | Main flags / meaning |
| --- | --- |
| `context status` | Read freshness, capture gaps, quotas and jobs; no indexing |
| `context search` | `--query`, `--limit`, `--max-bytes`, `--cursor`, repeated `--source-type`, `--since`, `--until` |
| `context read` | Repeated `--ref`, `--max-bytes`, `--cursor`; retained evidence, not current source |
| `context graph` | Repeated `--ref`, `--action` neighbors/path/timeline/related, `--target`, `--direction`, bounded `--max-hops`, `--max-nodes`, `--max-edges`, `--max-visited`, `--max-bytes`, `--wall-ms`, `--generation` |
| `context sync` | Explicit incremental indexing of retained events; no source scan |
| `context checkpoint` | `--summary`, repeated `--evidence-ref`; observed Git state, reported summary/claims |
| `context rebuild` | Explicit bounded derived-index rebuild |
| `context cancel` | `--job-ref`; scoped foreground/pending job semantics, not a persistent worker |

`mcp-device --help` exposes the generated command contract. Unknown flags/actions fail closed. Exit codes: 0 success; 2 invalid action/budget/scope; 3 permission/local-state/token denial; 4 missing/stale index/ref/cursor; 5 busy/quota/degraded storage; 1 unexpected internal failure. CLI calls ContextService directly and needs neither gateway nor network.

Search first, read selected refs, then inspect current files with native tools. Preserve returned scope, provenance, coverage, generation, retention/redaction and partial-result fields. Use continuations with the same query/refs; do not manufacture or repurpose cursors. Run sync only when required by the task and freshness, not on every turn. No status/read/search/idle scheduler exists.

## Public MCP schemas

The source of truth is [`../src/context/tool-contract.ts`](../src/context/tool-contract.ts), verified against the frozen six-tool fixture. Registration and live capture remain approval-gated; a local development version does not authorize production rollout.

| Tool | Lane | Operations |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `local_status` | read | Inspect state without maintenance |
| `local_search` | read | Bounded retained evidence retrieval |
| `local_read` | read | Selected refs/ranges within aggregate byte bounds |
| `local_graph` | read | Bounded evidence-backed activity relations |
| `local_index` | write | sync/checkpoint/rebuild/cancel, no destructive admin |
| `local_wiki` | read | status only, returns WIKI_DISABLED; zero provider calls |

Device/repository ownership and permissions are checked before ref/cursor/job lookup. Unsupported old devices return DEVICE_UNSUPPORTED rather than server fallback. Unknown public exceptions become INTERNAL_ERROR without sensitive details. Schema ceilings do not guarantee that every allowed input is implemented: unsupported graph continuations and maintenance orchestration inputs fail closed; domain graph limits are stricter than some schema maxima.

## Scope, privacy and limitations

Repository identity follows Git common-directory identity; worktrees share the repository, separate clones do not. Ambiguous/non-Git scopes fail rather than falling back globally. After re-pair, former owner namespaces remain sealed. ACCESS_DENIED/LOCAL_STATE_UNAVAILABLE is not permission to bypass a sandbox remotely.

See [PRIVACY.md](../PRIVACY.md): redaction is best-effort, local SQLite is not encrypted, and selected remote evidence traverses the gateway. No transcript reading, source-code parser, embedding provider, generated wiki or hidden worker is included. Activity edges describe observations/attempts, not causation or proof that an edit fixed a test. Capture gaps and unknown-after-restart remain explicit; no side-effect replay occurs. Process-crash tests do not prove host power-loss durability.

New captured evidence uses opaque 48-hex refs accepted by read and activity graph; existing migrations continue accepting legacy string/UUID refs. Observer-to-graph parity has a regression test. Benchmark capture and activity datasets remain separate and do not imply identical workloads.

## Approval trust and revocation

Phase 7/8 implemented and gated; production enablement pending SOC confirmation of fingerprint 45fa563f...d0d6a7c4 and private key custody.

Production pins `SOC_PUBLIC_KEY`; there is no environment, caller key or config-directory override. Test and benchmark positive controls substitute a throwaway key and installed-config file reads only in their isolated Node process using `syncBuiltinESMExports`; they do not replace the production pin. Approval artifacts authorize feature scopes, **not a device ID**. `expires_at` is an integer Unix timestamp in **milliseconds**. Anyone with write access to the installed config folder can modify the revocation list (including removing entries); filesystem access control is therefore part of the trust boundary. Missing, malformed or unreadable revocations fail closed. Every capture event rechecks authorization synchronously; revocation/expiry detaches the active observer before any write. Public context tools are hidden when authorization is locked, and locked device capability routing returns `DEVICE_UNSUPPORTED`; direct privileged entry calls throw `FEATURE_GATE_LOCKED`.

`src/device/gateway-*` is the existing device-management communication channel. Phase 8 public context dispatch/domain integration lives in `src/context/device-adapter.ts` and `src/context/gateway.ts`; the device tool adapter bridges these through `executePublicGateway`.

## Skill source and attribution

[`../skills/mcp-device-context/SKILL.md`](../skills/mcp-device-context/SKILL.md) is portable source shipped in the npm package. Load it explicitly through the client's supported mechanism; no installer or universal automatic invocation is promised. Exact validated clients/limitations are in `evidence/context/skill-compat.md`.

All new context code, documentation and skill source remain covered by the repository MIT [license](../LICENSE). No new third-party runtime dependency, copied external skill implementation, parser/model/provider or license obligation is introduced by Phase 9. SQLite is supplied by Node; Node/SQLite upstream license notices remain those of the installed runtime, not a bundled SQLite driver. The benchmark reuses this repository's accepted 1.0.10 benchmark core, attributed in `bench/README.md`.

## Evidence and delivery boundary

`evidence/context/platform-matrix.md`, `independent-review.md` and `acceptance.md` distinguish measured results, failures and NOT_MEASURED prerequisites. Benchmark measurements are local-domain fixture timings, not production network latency or release authorization. No merge, publish, tag or release is authorized by this work.

===== END SOURCE docs/context.md =====

===== BEGIN SOURCE docs/log-palace.md | original-bytes=3700 | sha256=6abd9f579d62590abbb1893aceaa24264290b93caac6b11a82d3901cf6fc7c62 =====
# Local log search (P1; source build)

This is a **CLI feature in the G6 Device source**. It does not require a live Context observer, authentication setup beyond existing OS filesystem rights, a Gateway MCP tool, or an LLM. This source change has **not** been published to npm or deployed.

## Commands

After building from the Device source:

~~~powershell
# Register standard existing logs (Pi, Codex, MCP tool history)
node dist/mcp-device.js logs discover

# Or add any file/folder explicitly
node dist/mcp-device.js logs add C:\path\to\session.jsonl --format auto
node dist/mcp-device.js logs add C:\path\to\custom.log --format text

# Index only when needed; append indexing is incremental
node dist/mcp-device.js logs index --max-files 200 --max-bytes 33554432

# Find compact refs (no raw log body), then read only the relevant record
node dist/mcp-device.js logs search --query "reconnect timeout" --limit 5
node dist/mcp-device.js logs read --ref <48-hex-ref> --max-bytes 8192
# When SEARCH returns anchor_status=raw_match, fetch the exact late hit in ONE read:
node dist/mcp-device.js logs read --ref <48-hex-ref> --from-byte <hit_offset> --max-bytes 8192

# Optional associations from IDs/file paths actually present in source logs
node dist/mcp-device.js logs graph --ref <48-hex-ref> --limit 12
node dist/mcp-device.js logs sources
~~~

When the package containing this feature is later installed, replace "node dist/mcp-device.js" with "mcp-device".

**Derived database:** by default under the same user's MCP_DEVICE_CONFIG_DIR/log-palace/index.sqlite. For safe smoke testing set MCP_DEVICE_LOG_PALACE_DIR to a dedicated empty directory before running the commands; the original source logs are never modified.

## What gets indexed?

- Pi session header, assistant toolCall IDs and corresponding toolResult events.
- Codex session metadata, function call/output and turn identities.
- MCP legacy history toolName/arguments/output.
- Unknown .jsonl/.log/.txt lines as **searchable text only**, not guessed graph edges.

The source record is referenced by file path, original byte offset, part index and SHA-256. Search returns a small locator; read reopens the source and verifies its hash. Graph edges represent observed matching call/session/turn or normalized full file identity, **never causal correctness**.

## Limits/important caveats

- Directory source discovery is bounded; repeatedly invoke index if more_files is true.
- Incomplete trailing JSONL records wait for a newline; giant lines advance resumably across bounded indexing calls. A complete record above 128 MiB fails explicitly with `RECORD_TOO_LARGE`.
- Search is Unicode FTS5 keyword/BM25, **not semantic cross-repo retrieval yet**.
- Raw files remain authoritative. File rotation/deletion may invalidate previous refs; a later index clears/rebuilds the derived entries.
- Model-visible text is UTF-8. Invalid UTF-8 source bytes fail explicitly instead of being silently replaced. `selected_part` is a bounded compatibility view and does not emit a continuation cursor; use a SEARCH `hit_offset` / raw-source read for resumable byte ranges.
- No new background tasks, extra redaction/capture gate, Wiki extraction or automatic skill processing occur. Cross-device sharing and Wiki are later phases.
- Source files and index DB contain potentially private information and remain on the owner's local machine. Do not attach private raw sessions to an external review service.

Tests: node --test test/log-palace/log-palace.test.js (after compiling TypeScript). For source status and known gaps see [Spec 006 handoff](../specs/006-agent-memory-palace/HANDOFF.md).
===== END SOURCE docs/log-palace.md =====

# CURRENT STATUS — MCP Device / Memory Palace

**Updated:** 2026-10-13 JST. P1 local source acceptance is complete at `e1f74715b60a008171d3e0a9c27c8f4f2964ce2c`; this is NOT a published, installed or deployed Device release. Verify actual git HEAD/status and runtime on the target host. This file describes the development checkout, not the installed binary or production gateway.

## Source of truth

- **G6 Device source:** E:\git-project\agent-mcp-device on main. Baseline P0 report eaa5192; P1 log-first CLI under src/log-palace/ is a later source-only increment. No npm publish, no rollout.
- **G8 Device:** separate checkout. No automatic synchronization or live Device upgrade is claimed by work on G6.
- **Gateway integration:** G8 /home/ayumi/Workspace/git_project/agent-mcp-gateway-develop on develop is canonical. The G6 Gateway checkout was aligned with G8 develop during prior work but is not production itself.
- Legacy Context v1.0.11 (six local_* tools, optional capture hooks) remains intact for compatibility. This new feature neither enables nor depends on that capture path.

## What actually works in G6 P1 source

The local command family **mcp-device logs** now provides:
1. Register/discover existing MCP tool history, Pi and Codex session logs or any user-selected log.
2. Incrementally index actual complete log lines into SQLite FTS5; no observer/extra capture gate.
3. Keyword search returning compact references, then bounded read of the exact original source bytes.
4. On-demand graph of **recorded** call IDs, session/turn IDs, and file identity verified by logged cwd.
5. Detect append, partial tail, replay, truncation/replacement, missing source and false same-file joins.

Only the derived index is written. Original logs are unchanged. This is a local CLI only, not a new MCP catalog tool.

**Source locations:** src/log-palace/{formats,store,cli}.ts and the CLI branch in src/mcp-device.ts. Test: test/log-palace/log-palace.test.js. See [CLI guide](log-palace.md) and [the takeover handoff](../specs/006-agent-memory-palace/HANDOFF.md).

### Verification

- **Accepted P1 implementation SHA:** `e1f74715b60a008171d3e0a9c27c8f4f2964ce2c`; prior indexing/fairness checkpoint `716f011` is historical. Independent Windows TEST at final SHA: Log Palace **39/39 PASS**, full Device (including build) **66/66 PASS**, targeted corrections **9/9 PASS**, own synthetic Pi provenance probe **10/10 PASS**. The same independent provenance probe on WSL Ubuntu 22.04/Node 22.21.0 **10/10 PASS**. Independent REVIEW turn 4 examined the final code and reran targeted **9/9** and Windows/WSL **10/10**, finding no actionable in-scope defect.
- Existing Context compatibility **10/10 PASS** in final DEV verification; Context consistency checker `passed=true` in independent TEST (not an independent product approval). SEARCH anchors are exact source-record-relative bytes when provable, including selected Pi part; otherwise `unmapped`. READ bounds **text bytes** (128–65,536), not the complete serialized JSON response.
- Latest isolated real-log benchmark: 3 authorized sources, 6,690,674 input bytes, 1,759 records; index p50/p95 ~902/1054 ms, search p50/p95 ~1.52/2.22 ms, raw hashes unchanged. See Spec 006 HANDOFF for RSS/storage/response-byte receipts and 10,020-file benchmark.
- Existing P0 historic audit is [P0-BASELINE-AUDIT.md](../specs/006-agent-memory-palace/P0-BASELINE-AUDIT.md). Its legacy capture observer bugs are NOT prerequisites to log-first P1.
- **P1 LOCATE→READ correction accepted in source** after DEV red/green, independent TEST and clean REVIEW on `e1f7471`: a 132,023-byte source with late hit is retrieved using one bounded selected READ; UTF-8 boundaries and cursors are valid; Pi part provenance is strict (mirror, ambiguous duplicate `message`/`content` keys and unmappable normalized terms do not invent anchors). Invalid source text fails explicitly rather than producing replacement characters. Concurrent-writer snapshot stress and fresh 64 MiB / 10,020-file performance reruns were **NOT_MEASURED on this final SHA**; earlier benchmarks remain historical. P1 is not published, deployed or synonymous with the future Wiki Palace.

## What is still NOT implemented

- LLM Wiki / ReMe/OpenViking backend, cross-device portable Palace and automatic no-guide new-repo recall.
- Agent-owned skill learning, lazy publication, scheduled merge and rollback.
- Unlimited single-record support is not implemented: complete records above 128 MiB fail explicitly with `RECORD_TOO_LARGE`. Compressed rotated logs are not indexed. Bounded >10,000-file discovery and p50/p95 benchmarks are implemented and measured.
- Additional authorization/secret gates are **not** intended for already-authorized local reads. If Wiki is later exported to another account/service, that separate transfer requires only the existing applicable access controls.

## Next agent

**P1 milestone closed in G6 source.** For the next separately authorized increment, start at [Spec 006 HANDOFF.md](../specs/006-agent-memory-palace/HANDOFF.md) and evaluate ONE Wiki backend (ReMe vs OpenViking) on a sanitized indexed corpus, with grounded citations and bounded LLM cost. Do not restart P1, reinstate capture/observer or start Pi/LLM background loops.

## Historical maintenance

- Spec 002 Device Local Context is DONE but retained as required regression fixture; old 001/003/004/005 retired and reachable in Git history. See [ARCHIVE.md](ARCHIVE.md).
- G8 production Gateway release and runtime are not changed by this work. No automatic release/merge from G6 to Gateway.
- User explicitly prefers reducing agent token cost and eliminating repeated guidance; do not preload logs/Wiki/skill bodies into prompt when a two-step locator suffices.
